- **refactoring-reconciler**: Invoked by the refactor skill's Workflow in the Mark and Reconcile phases — the only agent in that Workflow allowed to touch git. In Reconcile it runs the whole-project build gate, resets on failure, and isolates whether the intra-domain work itself stayed sound. Never edits code, never chooses what to apply — pure git and build mechanics only.
